  "components": {
    "securitySchemes": {
      "PortalSession": {
        "type": "apiKey",
        "in": "cookie",
        "name": "__Host-sikkerheit-session",
        "description": "Backend OIDC session, HttpOnly Secure SameSite; role, platform_role and organization checks on every request."
      },
      "CsrfToken": {
        "type": "apiKey",
        "in": "header",
        "name": "X-CSRF-Token",
        "description": "Required together with PortalSession for portal mutations."
      },
      "EnrollmentToken": {
        "type": "apiKey",
        "in": "header",
        "name": "Authorization",
        "description": "Enrollment <token>: organization-scoped single_use or batch token with expiry, maxUses and revocation; never written to logs or MSI."
      },
      "DeviceCertificate": {
        "type": "mutualTLS",
        "description": "Agent API runs in a dedicated Container Apps environment without other apps and is not an environment-level route target. The app accepts exactly one X-Forwarded-Client-Cert header, checks Hash = SHA-256 of Cert, builds the chain to the internal CA with its own trust (not the system store), requires the hash to be a registered, unrevoked device_certificate that is pending or active with validTo in the future, or superseded with overlapEndsAt in the future (24 h after the successor's first use, its validTo or the next issuance, whichever is first). Certificate state and revocation are read from the database on every request, without an authorization cache. A hash of a deleted organization's certificate is answered 401 certificate_revoked until its validTo. Organization and device IDs come only from the certificate."
      }
    },
    "schemas": {
      "ScanBatch": {
        "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Scan Batch",
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "schemaVersion": {
            "const": "1.0"
          },
          "scanId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "sequence": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "agentVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          },
          "policyVersion": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1,
            "description": "Версия политики источника."
          },
          "machineFingerprint": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 64,
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
          },
          "startedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
          },
          "completedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
          },
          "scanRequestId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$",
            "description": "Только вариант A. Чужой или просроченный scanRequestId не отклоняет пакет: он принимается как обычный сбор, scanRequestId игнорируется, пишется запись диагностики."
          },
          "observations": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Observation"
            },
            "minItems": 1,
            "maxItems": 200,
            "description": "Ключ (checkId, subject.type, localKey) уникален внутри пакета; localKey = device для устройства и volumeId для тома. Повтор ключа: 422 duplicate_observation."
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "schemaVersion",
          "scanId",
          "sequence",
          "agentVersion",
          "policyVersion",
          "machineFingerprint",
          "startedAt",
          "completedAt",
          "observations"
        ],
        "description": "DeviceId и OrganizationId определяются сертификатом, а не телом пакета. Лимит байтов, idempotency, sequence, окна и время проверяются сервером дополнительно. Внутренне противоречивый пакет (completedAt раньше startedAt, observedAt вне [startedAt, completedAt]) отклоняется 422 batch_time_inconsistent. startedAt, completedAt или observedAt позже receivedAt + 5 мин пакет не отклоняют: он принимается с признаком часов clock_inconsistent (05, «Время и часы устройства»). Метки времени внутри evidence при приёме не проверяются и оцениваются по правилам проверки (03)."
      },
      "Observation": {
        "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Observation",
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "checkId": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "WIN-001",
              "WIN-002",
              "WIN-003",
              "WIN-004",
              "WIN-005",
              "WIN-006",
              "WIN-007",
              "WIN-008",
              "WIN-009",
              "WIN-010",
              "WIN-011"
            ]
          },
          "collectorVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          },
          "subject": {
            "type": "object",
            "description": "Форма объекта задаётся по checkId (allOf)."
          },
          "observedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
          },
          "collectionStatus": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "collected",
              "unavailable"
            ]
          },
          "evidence": {
            "type": "object"
          },
          "reasonCode": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "permission_denied",
              "unsupported",
              "collector_timeout",
              "temporary_error"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "checkId",
          "collectorVersion",
          "subject",
          "observedAt",
          "collectionStatus"
        ],
        "oneOf": [
          {
            "properties": {
              "collectionStatus": {
                "const": "collected"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "evidence"
            ],
            "not": {
              "required": [
                "reasonCode"
              ]
            }
          },
          {
            "properties": {
              "collectionStatus": {
                "const": "unavailable"
              }
            },
            "required": [
              "reasonCode"
            ],
            "not": {
              "required": [
                "evidence"
              ]
            }
          }
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-001"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "volume"
                    },
                    "volumeId": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 64,
                      "pattern": "^\\\\\\\\\\?\\\\Volume\\{[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}\\}\\\\$"
                    },
                    "mountPoint": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 2,
                      "pattern": "^[A-Z]:$"
                    },
                    "role": {
                      "const": "os"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type",
                    "volumeId",
                    "role"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-002"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "volume"
                    },
                    "volumeId": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 64,
                      "pattern": "^\\\\\\\\\\?\\\\Volume\\{[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}\\}\\\\$"
                    },
                    "mountPoint": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 2,
                      "pattern": "^[A-Z]:$"
                    },
                    "role": {
                      "const": "os"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type",
                    "volumeId",
                    "role"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-003"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-004"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-005"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-006"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-007"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-008"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-009"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-010"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-011"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "subject": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "type": {
                      "const": "device"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "type"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-001"
                },
                "collectionStatus": {
                  "const": "collected"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId",
                "collectionStatus"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "evidence": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "totalBytes": {
                      "type": "integer",
                      "minimum": 1
                    },
                    "freeBytes": {
                      "type": "integer",
                      "minimum": 0
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "totalBytes",
                    "freeBytes"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-002"
                },
                "collectionStatus": {
                  "const": "collected"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId",
                "collectionStatus"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "evidence": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "volumeStatus": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "fully_encrypted",
                        "encrypting",
                        "decrypting",
                        "fully_decrypted"
                      ]
                    },
                    "protectionStatus": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "on",
                        "off"
                      ]
                    },
                    "encryptionPercentage": {
                      "type": "integer",
                      "minimum": 0,
                      "maximum": 100
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "volumeStatus",
                    "protectionStatus",
                    "encryptionPercentage"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-003"
                },
                "collectionStatus": {
                  "const": "collected"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId",
                "collectionStatus"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "evidence": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "products": {
                      "type": "array",
                      "items": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "additionalProperties": false,
                        "properties": {
                          "provider": {
                            "type": "string",
                            "minLength": 1,
                            "maxLength": 120,
                            "pattern": "^[^\\u0000-\\u001F\\u007F-\\u009F\\u200B-\\u200F\\u202A-\\u202E\\u2066-\\u2069<>]+$"
                          },
                          "productState": {
                            "type": "string",
                            "enum": [
                              "on",
                              "off",
                              "snoozed",
                              "expired",
                              "unknown"
                            ]
                          },
                          "statusSource": {
                            "type": "string",
                            "enum": [
                              "windows_security_center",
                              "defender"
                            ]
                          },
                          "rawState": {
                            "type": [
                              "integer",
                              "null"
                            ]
                          }
                        },
                        "required": [
                          "provider",
                          "productState",
                          "statusSource"
                        ],
                        "allOf": [
                          {
                            "if": {
                              "properties": {
                                "productState": {
                                  "const": "unknown"
                                }
                              },
                              "required": [
                                "productState"
                              ]
                            },
                            "then": {
                              "required": [
                                "rawState"
                              ],
                              "properties": {
                                "rawState": {
                                  "type": "integer"
                                }
                              }
                            }
                          }
                        ]
                      },
                      "minItems": 0,
                      "maxItems": 10,
                      "description": "Больше 10 продуктов: collectionStatus=unavailable, reasonCode=unsupported; список не усекается."
                    },
                    "defender": {
                      "type": [
                        "object",
                        "null"
                      ],
                      "additionalProperties": false,
                      "properties": {
                        "amRunningMode": {
                          "type": "string",
                          "enum": [
                            "normal",
                            "passive",
                            "edr_block",
                            "sxs_passive",
                            "not_running",
                            "other"
                          ]
                        },
                        "realTimeProtectionEnabled": {
                          "type": "boolean"
                        }
                      },
                      "required": [
                        "amRunningMode",
                        "realTimeProtectionEnabled"
                      ]
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "products",
                    "defender"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-004"
                },
                "collectionStatus": {
                  "const": "collected"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId",
                "collectionStatus"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "evidence": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "signatureLastUpdatedAt": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 40,
                      "format": "date-time",
                      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "signatureLastUpdatedAt"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-005"
                },
                "collectionStatus": {
                  "const": "collected"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId",
                "collectionStatus"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "evidence": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "domainEnabled": {
                      "type": "boolean"
                    },
                    "privateEnabled": {
                      "type": "boolean"
                    },
                    "publicEnabled": {
                      "type": "boolean"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "domainEnabled",
                    "privateEnabled",
                    "publicEnabled"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-006"
                },
                "collectionStatus": {
                  "const": "collected"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId",
                "collectionStatus"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "evidence": {
                  "type": "object",
                  "additionalProperties": false,
                  "properties": {
                    "configuredSource": {
                      "type": "string",
                      "enum": [
                        "windows_update",
                        "wsus",
                        "other_approved"
                      ],
                      "description": "Источник, к которому относится наблюдение. Сервер хранит историю ожидания по (deviceId, configuredSource, updateId): наблюдение источника обновляет и закрывает эпизоды только этого источника и задаёт previousCompleteAt только для него (03, «Правило WIN-006»)."
                    },
                    "auLastSearchSuccessAt": {
                      "type": [
                        "string",
                        "null"
                      ],
                      "minLength": 1,
                      "maxLength": 40,
                      "format": "date-time",
                      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$",
                      "description": "LastSearchSuccessDate, прочитанный до начала agentSearch; основа свежести при offline_cache, null — основы нет (03, «Правило WIN-006»)."
                    },
                    "agentSearch": {
                      "type": "object",
                      "additionalProperties": false,
                      "properties": {
                        "mode": {
                          "type": "string",
                          "enum": [
                            "offline_cache",
                            "online"
                          ]
                        },
                        "completedAt": {
                          "type": [
                            "string",
                            "null"
                          ],
                          "minLength": 1,
                          "maxLength": 40,
                          "format": "date-time",
                          "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
                        },
                        "resultCode": {
                          "type": "string",
                          "enum": [
                            "not_started",
                            "succeeded",
                            "succeeded_with_errors",
                            "failed",
                            "aborted"
                          ]
                        }
                      },
                      "required": [
                        "mode",
                        "completedAt",
                        "resultCode"
                      ],
                      "allOf": [
                        {
                          "if": {
                            "properties": {
                              "resultCode": {
                                "const": "succeeded"
                              }
                            },
                            "required": [
                              "resultCode"
                            ]
                          },
                          "then": {
                            "properties": {
                              "completedAt": {
                                "type": "string",
                                "minLength": 1,
                                "maxLength": 40,
                                "format": "date-time",
                                "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
                              }
                            }
                          }
                        }
                      ]
                    },
                    "pendingSecurityUpdates": {
                      "type": "array",
                      "items": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "additionalProperties": false,
                        "properties": {
                          "updateId": {
                            "type": "string",
                            "minLength": 1,
                            "maxLength": 36,
                            "format": "uuid",
                            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
                          },
                          "revisionNumber": {
                            "type": "integer",
                            "minimum": 0
                          },
                          "kbArticleIds": {
                            "type": "array",
                            "items": {
                              "type": "string",
                              "minLength": 1,
                              "maxLength": 8,
                              "pattern": "^[0-9]{6,8}$"
                            },
                            "minItems": 0,
                            "maxItems": 10,
                            "uniqueItems": true
                          },
                          "categoryIds": {
                            "type": "array",
                            "items": {
                              "type": "string",
                              "minLength": 1,
                              "maxLength": 36,
                              "format": "uuid",
                              "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
                            },
                            "minItems": 0,
                            "maxItems": 10,
                            "uniqueItems": true
                          },
                          "msrcSeverity": {
                            "type": [
                              "string",
                              "null"
                            ],
                            "enum": [
                              "Critical",
                              "Important",
                              "Moderate",
                              "Low",
                              null
                            ]
                          },
                          "lastDeploymentChangeAt": {
                            "type": [
                              "string",
                              "null"
                            ],
                            "minLength": 1,
                            "maxLength": 40,
                            "format": "date-time",
                            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
                          },
                          "deadlineAt": {
                            "type": [
                              "string",
                              "null"
                            ],
                            "minLength": 1,
                            "maxLength": 40,
                            "format": "date-time",
                            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
                          },
                          "firstSeenPendingAt": {
                            "type": "string",
                            "minLength": 1,
                            "maxLength": 40,
                            "format": "date-time",
                            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$",
                            "description": "observedAt первого сбора, в котором агент нашёл обновление у этого configuredSource; не позже observedAt наблюдения. Агент хранит значение по (configuredSource, updateId) и удаляет его только после поиска с resultCode=succeeded того же configuredSource без этого updateId."
                          }
                        },
                        "required": [
                          "updateId",
                          "revisionNumber",
                          "kbArticleIds",
                          "categoryIds",
                          "msrcSeverity",
                          "lastDeploymentChangeAt",
                          "deadlineAt",
                          "firstSeenPendingAt"
                        ]
                      },
                      "minItems": 0,
                      "maxItems": 50,
                      "description": "Только Security Updates по CategoryID, по возрастанию min(deadlineAt, firstSeenPendingAt); без заголовков.",
                      "uniqueItems": true
                    },
                    "pendingSecurityUpdatesTotal": {
                      "type": "integer",
                      "minimum": 0,
                      "description": "Длина pendingSecurityUpdates = min(total, 50); иначе 422 schema_violation."
                    },
                    "truncated": {
                      "type": "boolean",
                      "description": "true тогда и только тогда, когда pendingSecurityUpdatesTotal > 50; иначе 422 schema_violation."
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "configuredSource",
                    "auLastSearchSuccessAt",
                    "agentSearch",
                    "pendingSecurityUpdates",
                    "pendingSecurityUpdatesTotal",
                    "truncated"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "checkId": {
                  "const": "WIN-007"
                },
                "collectionStatus": {
                  "const": "collected"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "checkId",
                "collectionStatus"
              ]
            },
            "then": {

        "description": "Закрытые allowlisted evidence-структуры P1 для каждого checkId. Применимость определяет сервер: агент не передаёт not_applicable. Межполевые и семантические инварианты (freeBytes <= totalBytes, согласованность списка WIN-006, ожидаемые объекты, время) проверяет backend по 05-data-and-api.md и specs/invariants.json."
      },

        "description": "Закрытая конфигурация policy_revision. WIN-011 обязательна в activeCheckIds; assessed без входных настроек, отсутствие WIN-011 и активная WIN-004 без WIN-003 дают 422 policy_inputs_missing. maxBatchAgeDays >= maxBufferDays + 1, иначе 422 schema_violation. У каждого обязательного поля-значения есть default; объект по умолчанию составляется из default вложенных полей. Политика v1 по умолчанию: значения default этой схемы — все WIN активны, режимы равны defaultAssessmentMode каталога."
      },
      "PolicyRevision": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "version": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "effectiveFrom": {
            "type": "string",

            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "createdAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "createdBy": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "configuration": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/PolicyConfiguration"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "version",
          "effectiveFrom",
          "createdAt",
          "createdBy",
          "configuration"
        ]
      },

              "enable",
              "schedule_deletion",
              "cancel_deletion"
            ]
          },
          "reason": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 1000,
            "description": "Обязательный свободный текст; недоверенная строка. Сохраняется без изменений в details.reason записей аудита organization.lifecycle_change и выдаётся только как AuditEvent.details.reason."
          },
          "idempotencyKey": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "exportWaived": {
            "type": "boolean"
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "action",
          "reason",
          "idempotencyKey"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "action": {
                  "const": "schedule_deletion"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "action"
              ]
            },
            "else": {
              "not": {
                "required": [
                  "exportWaived"
                ]
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },

      "AuditEvent": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "occurredAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "actorType": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "user",
              "platform_admin",
              "device",
              "system"
            ]
          },
          "actorId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$",
            "description": "entraUserId при actorType user или platform_admin; deviceId при device; null при system."
          },
          "action": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 80,
            "pattern": "^[a-z_]+(\\.[a-z_]+)+$"
          },
          "targetType": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40
          },
          "targetId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "outcome": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "success",
              "denied",
              "failed"
            ]
          },
          "correlationId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 100
          },
          "details": {
            "type": [
              "object",
              "null"
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "occurredAt",
          "actorType",
          "actorId",
          "action",
          "targetType",
          "targetId",
          "outcome",
          "correlationId",
          "details"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "action": {
                  "const": "organization.lifecycle_change"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "action"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "details": {
                  "$ref": "#/components/schemas/OrganizationLifecycleAuditDetails"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "if": {
                "properties": {
                  "action": {
                    "const": "finding.assignment_change"
                  }
                },
                "required": [
                  "action"
                ]
              },
              "then": {
                "properties": {
                  "details": {
                    "$ref": "#/components/schemas/FindingAssignmentAuditDetails"
                  }
                }
              },
              "else": {
                "if": {
                  "properties": {
                    "action": {
                      "const": "retention.purge_summary"
                    }
                  },
                  "required": [
                    "action"
                  ]
                },
                "then": {
                  "properties": {
                    "details": {
                      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/RetentionPurgeAuditDetails"
                    }
                  }
                },
                "else": {
                  "properties": {
                    "details": {
                      "type": "null"
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]
      },
      "EnrollmentTokenRequest": {

      "Assessment": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "deviceId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "subject": {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Subject"
          },
          "checkId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40
          },
          "ruleId": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 80
          },
          "ruleVersion": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
          },
          "sourcePolicyVersion": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "evaluatedPolicyVersion": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "assessmentScope": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "historical",
              "current"
            ]
          },
          "assessmentMode": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "assessed",
              "informational"
            ]
          },
          "result": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "pass",
              "fail",
              "unknown",
              "not_applicable",
              null
            ]
          },
          "freshness": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "fresh",
              "stale"
            ]
          },
          "reasonCode": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "permission_denied",
              "unsupported",
              "collector_timeout",
              "temporary_error",
              "missing_observation",
              "clock_inconsistent",
              "unmapped_state",
              "update_search_failed",
              "update_search_outdated",
              "update_source_mismatch",
              "update_list_truncated",
              "update_age_unconfirmed",
              "os_version_unknown",
              "os_inventory_missing",
              "applicability_input_missing",
              "policy_not_applicable",
              "evidence_time_invalid",
              null
            ]
          },
          "late": {
            "type": "boolean"
          },
          "observedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "receivedAt": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "evidence": {
            "type": [
              "object",
              "null"
            ]
          },
          "evidenceAvailability": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "available",
              "expired"
            ]
          },
          "explanation": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 2000
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "deviceId",
          "subject",
          "checkId",
          "ruleId",
          "ruleVersion",
          "sourcePolicyVersion",
          "evaluatedPolicyVersion",
          "assessmentScope",
          "assessmentMode",
          "result",
          "freshness",
          "reasonCode",
          "late",
          "observedAt",
          "receivedAt",
          "evidence",
          "evidenceAvailability",
          "explanation"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "assessmentMode": {
                  "const": "informational"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "assessmentMode"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "type": "null"
                },
                "reasonCode": {
                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "enum": [
                    "permission_denied",
                    "unsupported",
                    "collector_timeout",
                    "temporary_error",
                    "missing_observation",
                    "clock_inconsistent",
                    "applicability_input_missing",
                    null
                  ]
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "result": {
                  "enum": [
                    "pass",
                    "fail"
                  ]
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "result"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "reasonCode": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ],
        "description": "Режим informational: result всегда null, finding не создаётся. reasonCode допускается при result unknown или not_applicable, а также у informational-оценки с result=null, когда данных нет: коды недоступности наблюдения, missing_observation, clock_inconsistent, applicability_input_missing; такая оценка получает result=null с той же причиной. Остальные причины правил (unmapped_state, update_*, os_version_unknown, os_inventory_missing, evidence_time_invalid, policy_not_applicable) informational-оценке не присваиваются: result=null, reasonCode=null. Признак часов действует на все оценки пакета: assessed — unknown и clock_inconsistent, informational — null и clock_inconsistent; applicability_input_missing применяется, только если у пакета нет признака часов."
      },

      "FindingEvent": {
        "type": "object",
        "additionalProperties": false,
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },

            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 40,
            "format": "date-time",
            "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$"
          },
          "eventType": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "opened",
              "resolved",
              "reopened",
              "quality_changed",
              "workflow_changed",
              "policy_changed",
              "object_retired"
            ]

          "cause": {
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "device_change",
              "rule_change",
              "policy_change",
              "applicability_change",
              "data_quality",
              "manual_action",
              "review_expired"
            ]
          },
          "assessmentId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "actorId": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "minLength": 1,
            "maxLength": 36,
            "format": "uuid",
            "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
          },
          "fromWorkflowState": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "new",
              "in_progress",
              "risk_accepted",
              "suppressed",
              null
            ]
          },
          "toWorkflowState": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "new",
              "in_progress",
              "risk_accepted",
              "suppressed",
              null
            ]
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "occurredAt",
          "eventType",
          "cause",
          "assessmentId",
          "actorId",
          "fromWorkflowState",
          "toWorkflowState"
        ],
        "allOf": [
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "opened"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "device_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                },

                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "resolved"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "device_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change",
                    "applicability_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {

                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {

                    "device_change",
                    "rule_change",
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "quality_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "data_quality"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "workflow_changed"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "manual_action",
                    "review_expired"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "policy_changed"

              },

              ]
            },
            "then": {

                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "policy_change"
                  ]
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "eventType": {
                  "const": "object_retired"
                }
              },
              "required": [
                "eventType"
              ]
            },
            "then": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "enum": [
                    "device_change",
                    "manual_action"
                  ]
                },
                "assessmentId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          },
          {
            "if": {
              "properties": {
                "cause": {
                  "const": "manual_action"
                }
              },

              ]
            },
            "then": {

                "actorId": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              }
            },
            "else": {
              "properties": {
                "actorId": {
                  "type": "null"
                }
              }
            }
          }
        ]

            "enum": [
              "html"
            ]
          },
          "failureCode": {
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ],
            "enum": [
              "translation_missing",
              "generation_error",
              null
            ],
            "description": "translation_missing — нет перевода; generation_error — сбой генерации по иной причине, чем translation_missing."
          }
        },
        "required": [
          "id",
          "periodStart",
          "periodEnd",
          "timezone",
          "language",
          "state",
          "format",
          "failureCode"
        ]
      },

Файл `specs/scan-batch.schema.json`

```json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://contracts.sikkerheit.example.invalid/agent/scan-batch.schema.json",
  "title": "Sikkerheit Agent Scan Batch",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "properties": {
    "schemaVersion": {
      "const": "1.0"
    },
    "scanId": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 36,
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$"
    },
    "sequence": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "agentVersion": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "pattern": "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(?:[-+][A-Za-z0-9.-]+)?$"
    },
    "policyVersion": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1,
      "description": "Версия политики источника."
    },
    "machineFingerprint": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 64,
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-f]{64}$"
    },
    "startedAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "format": "date-time",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
    },
    "completedAt": {
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 40,
      "format": "date-time",
      "pattern": "^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,6})?Z$"
    },
    "scanRequestId": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "minLength": 1,
      "maxLength": 36,
      "format": "uuid",
      "pattern": "^[0-9a-fA-F]{8}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}$",
      "description": "Только вариант A. Чужой или просроченный scanRequestId не отклоняет пакет: он принимается как обычный сбор, scanRequestId игнорируется, пишется запись диагностики."
    },
    "observations": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "observation.schema.json"
      },
      "minItems": 1,
      "maxItems": 200,
      "description": "Ключ (checkId, subject.type, localKey) уникален внутри пакета; localKey = device для устройства и volumeId для тома. Повтор ключа: 422 duplicate_observation."
    }
  },
  "required": [
    "schemaVersion",
    "scanId",
    "sequence",
    "agentVersion",
    "policyVersion",
    "machineFingerprint",
    "startedAt",
    "completedAt",
    "observations"
  ],
  "description": "DeviceId и OrganizationId определяются сертификатом, а не телом пакета. Лимит байтов, idempotency, sequence, окна и время проверяются сервером дополнительно. Внутренне противоречивый пакет (completedAt раньше startedAt, observedAt вне [startedAt, completedAt]) отклоняется 422 batch_time_inconsistent. startedAt, completedAt или observedAt позже receivedAt + 5 мин пакет не отклоняют: он принимается с признаком часов clock_inconsistent (05, «Время и часы устройства»). Метки времени внутри evidence при приёме не проверяются и оцениваются по правилам проверки (03)."
}
```
